# RUNLOG — 2026-09

2026-09-03 17:14 EDT - published the public nonprofit Marketplace documentation phase.

- Added `/documentation/marketplace` as an anonymous, free-member, and paid-member-compatible U.S.-wide directory inside the shared documentation canvas and rail. The page includes answer-first guidance, four stage-specific decision tests, an explicit review method, canonical metadata, and matching `CollectionPage`, `ItemList`, and `BreadcrumbList` structured data.
- Published fifteen source-backed entries spanning Coach House coaching, nonprofit software, technology discounts, federal funding, tax-exempt organization guidance, fundraising research and learning, volunteer recruitment, board governance, and skills-based professional support. Each entry includes a narrow description, use case, access model, eligibility, geography, delivery, account, language, accessibility, official source, exact review date, recheck date, inclusion rationale, and related documentation where useful.
- Added five shareable URL-backed filters, deterministic results, a truthful empty state, expandable fit and provenance details, and a versioned device-local shortlist with clear/remove controls, persistence, and formula-safe CSV export. The shortlist is a comparison aid and does not rank, endorse, apply, purchase, approve, or predict fit.
- Added a coaching section that exposes only the first-party Coach House service. Individual coaches remain private until explicit public-profile opt-in, field-level publication, and current availability controls exist; active internal coaching records are not treated as public consent.
- Added a Community preview derived only from organizations already published on Map. The projection retains a public name, slug, tagline or description, coarse location, delivery mode, public group, and public program count while excluding private profiles, email, phone, representatives, street and postal addresses, coordinates, social handles, unpublished programs, imported candidates, and raw source queues. Local development currently shows the truthful empty state because no service-role-backed public cohort is available in this worktree.
- Promoted Marketplace in the documentation rail and home Resources section, linked CRM forward to the canonical page, preserved Map at `/`, and left the separate authenticated `/marketplace` unchanged. Replaced endorsement-adjacent “vetted” language with “source-backed.”
- Anonymous local Chromium QA passed at 1440 × 1000 light and 390 × 844 dark for 15 initial results, the two-entry software filter, shareable URL state, zero-result recovery, shortlist add/save/reload, CSV download, responsive cards, Community presentation, 16-pixel mobile search input, zero page overflow, and zero application console errors. The shared browser connection was unavailable, so the page could not be opened in the user's browser; the local route remains available on port 3010.
- An initial production build caught one missing type-only import after the catalog was split under the source-line budget; it was corrected. The canonical rerun passed all 21 `pnpm check:quality` stages: 421 acceptance files passed with one intentional skip, 2,282 tests passed with one skip, RLS checks passed, the production build passed, all 34 visual tests passed, and performance budgets passed. The build retained one pre-existing finance-plan tracing warning unrelated to documentation.
- Refreshed Graphify to 17,982 nodes, 51,346 edges, and 965 communities. No push, pull request, merge, deployment, external message, provider application, purchase, or public-profile mutation occurred. This completes all 20 planned documentation objectives; continue with editorial or catalog expansion only after product review.
